# Direct GZRAM policy and block comparisons

Physical N100 warm-cache userspace screening. Nine paired rounds per session; median of three session changes. Session ranges are descriptive, not confidence intervals. Negative changes mean less time/space/energy. Comparisons across block sizes can change encoding choices and ratios. PMU effects in JSON require fully scheduled user-only groups. No policy or kernel gain accepted.
